# fate-suite sweep triage

2221 files, 2411 scenarios (one per video / audio stream), reference ffmpeg n8.1.2. g2g-launch is g2g master 42c0a809 built with linux-full in release. The class comes from `calliope sweep`. The diagnosis comes from rules over the g2g error and the stream layout, checked by rerunning representative files against ffmpeg and gstreamer. Every g2g diverges row was rerun by hand. Reproducers read the sample tree from `$HOME/.cache/calliope/fate-suite`.

| class | scenarios |
| :--- | ---: |
| g2g diverges | 40 |
| g2g no output | 485 |
| gstreamer diverges | 130 |
| reference quirk | 18 |
| unsupported codec | 790 |
| agree | 948 |

Every scenario outside the agree class, by diagnosis:

| diagnosis | scenarios |
| :--- | ---: |
| g2g bug | 176 |
| unsupported | 1109 |
| harness | 98 |
| reference quirk | 38 |
| gstreamer only | 42 |

Bug 21 rows are also counted under unsupported, the codec is missing either way.

## g2g bugs

Each bug names one reproducer. `S=$HOME/.cache/calliope` in every command. The tables below list every affected scenario with its own reproducer. "Expected" is what ffmpeg n8.1 and gstreamer 1.26 do with the same file.

1. **decodebin picks the stream without looking at the downstream element.** On a file with audio and video, `decodebin ! audioconvert` builds the video chain, so audio is never decoded. Mkv files with MJPEG video and FLAC or Opus audio show the reverse: `decodebin ! videoconvert` gets the audio chain. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/filter/cropdetect.mp4 ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! fakesink` (H.264 + AAC). Expected: the AAC track decodes. Got: decodebin plugs FfmpegVideoDec and the run fails with `AudioConvert produces no output for video/x-raw,format=I420`. Same on `gst-medias/defaults/webm/vorbis_vp8.0.webm`, `gst-medias/defaults/bipbop/gear1/fileSequence0.ts` and, reversed, `gst-medias/defaults/matroska/flac_jpeg.0.mkv` with `! videoconvert !`.
2. **A demuxer drops a stream g2g can decode and its default H.264 port leaks.** Audio-only MP4, MOV and WebM files whose codec the container hook does not map get Mp4Demux or MkvDemux plus FfmpegVideoDec. An AVI with an MJPG fourcc gets AviDemux and no decoder. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/vp8/dash_audio1.webm ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! fakesink` (Vorbis only). Expected: Vorbis decodes. Got: MkvDemux ! FfmpegVideoDec and a caps error. Also `fate-suite/mpegaudio/packed_maindata.mp3.mp4` (MP3), `fate-suite/qt-surge-suite/surge-2-16-L-sowt.mov` (PCM s16le), `fate-suite/mov/aac-2048-priming.mov` (AAC) and `gst-medias/defaults/avi/bowlerhatdancer.sleepytom.SGP.mjpeg.avi` (MJPEG, `! videoconvert ! video/x-raw,format=I420 !`).
3. **Typefind takes non-H.264 data for an H.264 elementary stream.** An ID3-tagged ADTS file and MXF, hxvs and RealMedia files get NalParse ! FfmpegVideoDec instead of an unrecognized-content error or the right demuxer. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/aac/id3v2.aac ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! fakesink`. Expected: ADTS AAC behind the ID3 tag decodes. Got: NalParse ! FfmpegVideoDec, then a caps error. MXF example: `fate-suite/mxf/C0023S01.mxf` fails CapsMismatch in NalParse0. Raw MPEG-4 `fate-suite/mpeg4/demo.m4v` is typefound as MP4 and fails as in bug 4.
4. **Mp4Demux fails CapsMismatch on MP4 and MOV streams g2g decodes elsewhere.** Affects H.264, HEVC, MPEG-4, AV1, VP8 and MJPEG tracks, including plain single-track files. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/mov/displaymatrix.mov ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! fakesink` (H.264 160x240, one track). Expected: the frames decode. Got: `pipeline error in Mp4Demux0: CapsMismatch` after the first mid-stream caps, also on the retry without the stream. Same on `fate-suite/mov/white_zombie_scrunch-part.mov`, `fate-suite/mpeg4/fragmented.mp4`, `fate-suite/mov/dovi-p5.mp4`, `gst-medias/defaults/mp4/timecoded_vp8_30fps.mp4` and `gst-medias/defaults/exMPD_BIP_TC1/ElephantsDream_H264BPL30_0100.mp4`.
5. **Mp4Demux ignores edit lists.** Frames the edit list cuts are emitted. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/mov/mov_neg_first_pts_discard.mov ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! filesink location=/tmp/out.yuv`. Expected: 15 frames. Got: all 30 samples. `mov_stream_shorter_than_movie.mov` gives 48 for 24, `elst_ends_betn_b_and_i.mp4` and `mov-1elist-ends-last-bframe.mov` one frame past the edit end, `mov_ibi_elst_starts_b.mov` one extra leading frame, `mp4-init-nonkeyframe.mp4` 2 extra at the end after 27 matching frames.
6. **Mp4Demux uses only the first sample description.** `fate-suite/h264/extradata-reload-multi-stsd.mov` and its HEVC twin switch avcC / hvcC at frame 2. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/h264/extradata-reload-multi-stsd.mov ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! filesink location=/tmp/out.yuv`. Expected: 4 frames matching ffmpeg. Got: frames 0 and 1 match, frames 2 and 3 match no engine. gstreamer also fails this file.
7. **MP4 AAC decodes to nothing and g2g-launch exits 0.** AacParse forwards bogus caps from the raw MP4 access units (`channels=6,rate=7350`, then `rate=32000`, `rate=64000` for a mono 44.1 kHz file) and FfmpegAudioDec emits no samples. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/aac/al04_44.mp4 ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! audio/x-raw,format=S16LE,rate=44100,channels=1 ! filesink location=/tmp/out.pcm`. Expected: 355 frames of mono AAC. Got: a 0-byte file and exit status 0. 18 of the fate-suite AAC conformance files do this.
8. **ER AAC, AAC-ELD and USAC in MP4 fail decoder setup.** libavcodec logs `channel element 3.15 is not allocated` and FfmpegAudioDec returns Hardware(Other). ffmpeg decodes the same files with the same libavcodec, so the AudioSpecificConfig g2g passes is wrong. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/aac/er_eld2000np_48_ep0.mp4 ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! fakesink`. Expected: AAC-ELD stereo 48 kHz decodes. Got: `pipeline error in FfmpegAudioDec0: Hardware(Other)`.
9. **WavParse fails on valid PCM WAV files.** Files with extra RIFF chunks (LIST before data, bext, trailing cue / list) fail CapsMismatch in audioresample, and WAVE_FORMAT_EXTENSIBLE 24 and 32 bit files fail in audioconvert. The decoded output also includes the bytes of the chunks after `data`. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/wav/200828-005.wav ! wavparse ! audioresample ! fakesink`. Expected: 48 kHz stereo s16 plays. Got: `pipeline error: CapsMismatch`. `decodebin ! filesink` on the same file writes 2458080 bytes for a 2457856-byte data chunk. Also `fate-suite/audio-reference/chorusnoise_2ch_44kHz_s16.wav` (LIST chunk) and `fate-suite/audio-reference/divertimenti_2ch_96kHz_s24.wav` (extensible s24).
10. **MkvDemux merges two audio tracks into one stream.** Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/mkv/flac_channel_layouts.mka ! decodebin ! audioconvert dithering=none ! audioresample ! audio/x-raw,format=S16LE,rate=48000,channels=6 ! filesink location=/tmp/out.pcm`. Expected: 48128 samples per channel from the first FLAC track. Got: 96256, the first 48128 bit-exact with ffmpeg. The file has two 6-channel FLAC tracks.
11. **mkv/1242-small.mkv decodes 4 of 12 frames.** Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/mkv/1242-small.mkv ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! filesink location=/tmp/out.yuv` (H.264 1280x718). Expected: 12 frames. Got: 4 frames, ffmpeg's frames 1, 1, 2, 3, so frame 0 is missing and frame 1 repeats.
12. **Field-coded MPEG-2 in TS decodes wrong.** Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/mpeg2/mpeg2_field_encoding.ts ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! filesink location=/tmp/out.yuv` (720x576 field pictures, bottom field first). Expected: 31 frames bit-exact with ffmpeg and gstreamer, which agree. Got: 31 frames, none matches either.
13. **PsDemux stops after the first frame of mpeg2/t.mpg.** Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/mpeg2/t.mpg ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! filesink location=/tmp/out.yuv` (MPEG-2 video + MP3). Expected: 352 frames. Got: 1 frame, PsDemux0 processes 21 packets and the run ends with exit 0.
14. **FfmpegVideoDec fails NotConfigured on streams it lists as supported.** VP6 and VP6A in FLV, MJPEG in Matroska, H.264 in `h264/direct-bff.mkv`, MPEG-4 in `iv8/zzz-partial.mpg`, HEVC in `mpegts/loewe.ts`. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/flash-vp6/clip1024.flv ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! fakesink`. Expected: VP6F decodes. Got: `pipeline error in FfmpegVideoDec0: NotConfigured`, twice.
15. **FfmpegVideoDec fails Hardware(Other) on H.264 that starts without parameter sets or mixes NAL framing.** libavcodec logs `non-existing PPS 0 referenced` or `no frame!` and g2g aborts where ffmpeg skips to the next decodable frame. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/h264/h264_intra_first-small.ts ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! fakesink`. Expected: frames from the first decodable picture on. Got: `pipeline error in FfmpegVideoDec0: Hardware(Other)`. Also the later HLS segments `gst-medias/defaults/bipbop/gear2/fileSequence4.ts` and `fate-suite/h264/mixed-nal-coding.mp4`.
16. **NalParse fails CapsMismatch on a raw H.264 file.** Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/h264/crew_cif.nal ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! fakesink`. Expected: CIF frames, gstreamer decodes it. Got: `pipeline error in NalParse0: CapsMismatch`.
17. **StillImageParse fails CapsMismatch on JPEG that ffmpeg and gstreamer decode.** Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/jpg/20242.jpg ! decodebin ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! fakesink` (baseline yuvj420p). Expected: one frame. Got: `pipeline error in StillImageParse0: CapsMismatch`. Also MJPEG in `fate-suite/mjpeg/mjpeg_field_order.avi`.
18. **Multichannel Opus fails in OpusDec.** Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/opus/tron.6ch.tinypkts.mka ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! fakesink`. Expected: 6-channel Opus decodes. Got: `pipeline error in OpusDec0: CapsMismatch`. Same for 7.1 in `opus/test-8-7.1.opus-small.ts`.
19. **FlacParse splits frames wrong.** libavcodec reports `invalid residual` on a frame g2g hands it and the run stops. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/cover_art/cover_art.flac ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! fakesink`. Expected: FLAC s16 decodes, as ffmpeg and gstreamer do. Got: `pipeline error in FfmpegAudioDec0: Hardware(Other)`. Also `gst-medias/defaults/flac/samples.multimedia.cx_flac_Yesterday.flac`, which has no picture.
20. **OggDemux plugs OpusDec for any Ogg file with a video stream.** A Theora + Vorbis file decodes to nothing and exits 0, and a video request gets OpusDec. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/gst-medias/defaults/ogg/vorbis_theora.0.ogg ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! audio/x-raw,format=S16LE ! filesink location=/tmp/out.pcm`. Expected: the Vorbis track decodes. Got: OggDemux ! OpusDec and a 0-byte file.
21. **Unsupported codecs fail with a caps error instead of naming the missing decoder.** When no demuxer port maps the stream, decodebin plugs nothing or a placeholder chain and the run ends in `no caps overlap` or `CapsMismatch`, so the user cannot tell a missing codec from a negotiation bug. Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/aasc/AASC-1.5MB.AVI ! decodebin ! videoconvert ! video/x-raw,format=I420 ! fakesink`. Expected: `no decoder chain` for AASC. Got: `VideoConvert produces no output for video/x-h264`. The diagnosis column marks these rows "reported as a caps error (bug 21)". Low severity, one fix in decodebin covers them.
22. **decodebin has no chain from PCM U8 to raw in AVI.** Reproducer: `g2g-launch -q filesrc location=$S/fate-suite/cvid/pcitva15.avi ! decodebin ! audioconvert ! fakesink` (11025 Hz mono pcm_u8). Expected: the PCM plays. Got: `no decoder chain from Audio { format: PcmU8, ... } to raw`. The sweep files these as unsupported codec.
